# MCP Interactions Journal - 2026-07 - codex

**Timezone convention:** all times in this journal are founder local time (PDT/PST, America/Los_Angeles) unless explicitly noted. Source data stores ISO 8601 UTC; entries here are converted on write.

### 2026-07-15 22:31 PDT - read-only convergence audit of the active Codex review session

- **Trigger:** Founder asked Codex to connect through ECHO to the Codex review session and determine whether review rounds are doing substantive work and converging.
- **Query inputs:** `echo_resolve_mru({sources:["codex"], repo_path:"/Users/zhenye/Desktop/Project_echo"})`; `search_memories({source:"fs:/Users/zhenye/.codex/sessions/2026/07/15/rollout-2026-07-15T22-28-26-019f6965-c86c-7943-bd6f-8436475ea5b9.jsonl", repo_path:"/Users/zhenye/Desktop/Project_echo", limit:20})`; `find_clusters({repo_path:"/Users/zhenye/Desktop/Project_echo", since:"2026-07-15T21:45:00-07:00", format:"skeleton", view:"rich"})`; `search_memories({source_app:"codex", repo_path:"/Users/zhenye/Desktop/Project_echo", since:"2026-07-15T21:45:00-07:00", limit:50})`; four literal `search_memories` calls, one per full item ID 136-139, with `source_app:"codex"`, the same repo path, `since:"2026-07-15T22:15:00-07:00"`, and `limit:12`; follow-up `find_clusters({repo_path:"/Users/zhenye/Desktop/Project_echo", since:"2026-07-15T22:28:00-07:00", format:"skeleton", view:"rich"})` plus `get_atoms` over the latest 50 IDs using `prefer:"newest_first"` and projected `content,metadata`.
- **Returned:** MRU resolved one exact repo-scoped Codex session with no warnings. Its tail returned 14/14 turns, no truncation cursor or warnings: the parent session created a remote convergence task for specs 136-139 with a locked no-build endpoint, but the tail contains no remote worker round activity. Cluster discovery returned one untruncated dense cross-tool cluster (`ctx_26d582bf`) spanning 21:45-22:28 PDT with 148 atoms: 133 Codex, 10 git, 5 Claude Code; rank reasons `recent_activity`, `has_open_loop`, `code_session_anchor`, `dense`, `cross_tool`. The repo-wide Codex search returned 50 matches plus a continuation cursor and no warnings. It separated two substantive reviewer sessions: item 136's R9 audit passed pinned SHA `19449fd0c8a57f132ad11e87a786ef36ae12d450` after explicitly checking both R8 corrections plus tag recovery, manifest-hash carrier, main-only policy, deterministic ZIP identity, secret redaction, and migration-evidence wording; item 137's R4 audit rejected the current SHA with seven concrete residuals across PID truth, bootstrap integrity ordering, launchd log bounds, candidate invocation, durable failure evidence, owner-sidecar durability, and missing asset/bootstrap tests. The recorded 69- and 98-tool-call reviewer turns are consistent with substantive source/spec inspection, not bare verdict emission.
- **Sources:** MRU exact source `fs:/Users/zhenye/.codex/sessions/2026/07/15/rollout-2026-07-15T22-28-26-019f6965-c86c-7943-bd6f-8436475ea5b9.jsonl`; item 136 reviewer source `fs:/Users/zhenye/.codex/sessions/2026/07/15/rollout-2026-07-15T21-54-51-019f6947-0994-7cd0-9103-0a46c8779cd4.jsonl`; item 137 reviewer source `fs:/Users/zhenye/.codex/sessions/2026/07/15/rollout-2026-07-15T22-05-30-019f6950-7724-71e3-bf3a-139629e7f30f.jsonl`; cluster source breakdown `codex:133`, `git:10`, `claude_code:5`, all repo-scoped.
- **Verdict:** partial - ECHO correctly located the handoff/parent session, but this source alone proves only that the task was queued, not that the remote reviewer is working or converging.
- **Note:** Read-only audit; no coord emit/invoke or review artifact mutation. Exact-source separation matters because the MRU session is the parent handoff while the reviewer work lives in other Codex session files. On-disk pinned review artifacts will be used to verify item-by-item convergence, especially 138 and 139.
- **Conjecture:** The four late literal searches each returned only the same three parent/handoff turns (no cursor or warnings), despite canonical review commits advancing through 22:35 PDT. That looks like indexing lag and/or literal-query bias toward turns naming all four items, so ECHO is useful for locating sessions and inspecting completed reviewer turns but should not be treated as the live convergence ledger. The pinned request/response/combined artifacts and current `origin/main` object graph are the authoritative corroboration.
- **Follow-up returned:** The refreshed cluster (`ctx_ccbd5b9b`) covered 22:28:08-22:36:54 PDT with 50 untruncated membership IDs (`codex:49`, `claude_code:1`) and rank reasons `recent_activity`, `has_open_loop`, `dense`. `get_atoms` hit the response envelope after 11 newest atoms and deterministically dropped 39; the two newest target-worker tails were content-truncated but retained their conclusions. Session `...T22-28-08-019f6965-8516-7e02-bff5-d4f21d63dc6f.jsonl` performed 38 tool calls and found item 136 R10 still not converged: mutable local tag-ref rather than immutable tag-object OID, no proven durable Actions write-ahead marker across runner loss, and incomplete per-asset trace/stop fixtures. Session `...T22-28-20-019f6965-b39c-7c71-abc1-2045c650d23e.jsonl` performed 37 tool calls and found item 137 R4 ready for strategist disposition with nine deduplicated patch groups and a mandatory reframe gate, explicitly recommending R5 rather than promotion.
